# Skills · P · part 169

790 entries, prd-pipeline to prd-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prd-pipeline**: USE FOR: product requirements, implementation-ready planning, and filesystem-first product handoff. ([grant-vine/wunderkind](https://github.com/grant-vine/wunderkind/tree/HEAD/codex-src/skills/prd-pipeline) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **prd-pipeline**: Decompose validated idea-brief.md into 4–8 per-feature PRDs with screens nested. Use after idea-pipeline. Triggers — write PRD, decompose features, feature breakdown, screen list, MVP scope to PRD, post-idea-validation, per-feature PRD, product requirements document, generate PRDs. ([decoutkhanqindev/Aodamn](https://github.com/decoutkhanqindev/Aodamn/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prd-pipeline))
- **prd-pipeline**: 完整 PRD 工作流编排 —— 调研、结构化讨论、生成、审查、交付。依赖 prd-pipeline MCP server。 ([haonan-di/prd-pipeline](https://github.com/haonan-di/prd-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skill))
- **prd-pipeline**: Orchestrates a multi-agent pipeline that takes a PRD through analysis, parallel research, synthesis, design system, screen design, code generation and an accessibility audit. Use this skill whenever the user has a PRD, spec, requirements doc or feature brief and wants to turn it into designs, a prototype, a component library or working screens — and whenever they mention multi-agent workflows, agent orchestration, or parallelising design and build work. Use it even if they do not say "pipeline". ([shamimhasan/prd-to-product](https://github.com/shamimhasan/prd-to-product/tree/HEAD/plugins/prd-to-product/skills/prd-pipeline))
- **prd-pipeline**: 当用户要求把一个产品想法、需求草稿、issue、会议纪要或 PRD 从“调查研究/需求讨论/澄清”一路串到“完整 PRD、PRD 审计、工程交接、QA 测试用例”时使用本总控技能；适用于端到端、从想法到研发开工、从 PRD 到 tickets/测试矩阵等场景。必须先像 autoresearch 一样建立 research mission、sandbox、result，再像 gsd-discuss-phase/deep-interview 一样分析产品灰区、用 Codex 原生结构化选择 UI 逐轮追问、累计歧义评分，并维护 pipeline state 支持恢复。审计有 P0 阻塞时必须回环修 PRD，最终必须通过 completion artifact 验证。不要用于只写代码或只处理单一步骤。 ([xyjk0511/elite-prd-skill-pack](https://github.com/xyjk0511/elite-prd-skill-pack/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/prd-pipeline))
- **prd-plan**: Iteratively plan a Product Requirements Document (PRD) with the user via in-file checkbox Q&A, folding answers into the spec until signed off. Use when the user asks to "plan PRD-XXX", "create a PRD for X", "work on PRD-003", "iterate on PRD-XXX", or to turn a rough product idea into a fully specified PRD. PRDs are numbered sequentially (PRD-001, PRD-002, …). ([imjackofitall/open-coding-tools](https://github.com/imjackofitall/open-coding-tools/tree/HEAD/product-requirements) · ★1)
- **prd-plan**: Break an approved PRD/spec phase into bite-sized TDD implementation tasks: exact file paths, copy-pasteable code, exact test commands, RED-GREEN-REFACTOR ordering, commit-per-task, smoke-test-as-a-step. The serial single-executor (you or one subagent via subagent-driven-development) counterpart to prd-swarm-plan. Use after prd-spec + prd-review-pipeline, on approved phases, before building. (Formerly 'writing-plans'.) ([ANG-Ventures/agent-skills](https://github.com/ANG-Ventures/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/prd-plan))
- **prd-plan**: 승인된 PRD를 실행 계획(PLAN)으로 전개하는 워크플로. 요구사항(REQ)을 작업(TASK)으로 분해해 WBS·선행 의존 그래프·마일스톤·리스크 등록부를 만들고, 모든 TASK에 근거 REQ를 붙여 추적성을 보장한다. 절대 날짜와 임의 공수를 만들지 않고 상대 규모(S/M/L/XL)와 상대 순번만 쓴다. 작성은 prd-writer, 검수는 쓰기 권한 없는 PLAN 전용 검수자 prd-plan-reviewer가 맡고 최대 3라운드 반영 루프를 돈다. 사용자가 '실행 계획 세워줘', 'PLAN 문서 만들어줘', 'WBS 작성', '작업 분해', '개발 계획 수립', '프로젝트 계획서', '/prd-plan &lt;PRD경로&gt;' 등을 요청할 때 적용. 일정·완료 시점을 묻는 요청 — '일정표 만들어줘', '언제 끝나', '언제까지 되나', '얼마나 걸려', '납기', '납기 맞출 수 있나', '일정 산정', '공수 산정', '견적', '완료 시점', '스케줄 잡아줘' — 에도 같은 스킬이 적용된다. 이때 근거 없는 캘린더 날짜·시간 단위 공수·인원 배정을 지어내 답하지 않는다: 일정 환산에 필요한 입력 세 가지(① 착수일 ② 가용 인원·역할 ③ 외부 납기·리드타임… ([pjy4617/pjy-claude-skill](https://github.com/pjy4617/pjy-claude-skill/tree/HEAD/plugins/prd-plan/skills/prd-plan))

- **prd-prototype**: 一站式产出“交互原型 + 需求文档”的单 HTML：从需求材料或几句话出发，先轻量澄清并确认范围清单，再生成可点击的线框或高保真原型（视觉由模型按产品自行设计，不绑定任何公司规范），附页面状态、核心流程演示、功能点标注和全文需求文档；也能为已有 HTML 原型补齐需求标注。构建时自动对账，原型里多出需求没有的功能、需求缺少交互说明/字段定义/异常处理时都会阻止交付。用户提到做原型、线框图、高保真原型、交互原型、需求文档、PRD、原型标注、需求评审、给已有 HTML 补需求、基于上一版迭代 V1.1 时使用。需求还没定、只想先让人看到产品样子，或用户说“只要原型”“先出原型”时，也用它只出原型（原型阶段，不写需求说明）。 ([jianghaotj/PrdPrototype](https://github.com/jianghaotj/PrdPrototype/tree/HEAD/prd-prototype))

- **prd-qa-analyzer**: Standard operating procedure for performing deep, domain-agnostic Product Requirement Document (PRD) analysis, extracting functional & non-functional requirements, enforcing strict interactive validation gates (no wild assumptions), applying SFDIPOT heuristic test strategy, modeling state invariants, RBAC/security matrices, decision tables, and preparing evidence-grade analysis summary documents ready for test case generation. - **prd-quality**: Score two or more PRD files independently against the spec-lane delivery bar, then compare. Optional companion /prd-critic is secondary and cannot override a material FAIL. Writes reports_dir/{INIT}-prd-quality.md. Never edits the PRDs. Never run /prd, /prd-think, /prd-impact-map, or forge. - **prd-quality-gate**: Use before committing scope, estimate, or engineering time to any feature. - **prd-quality-guard**: Evalúa la calidad de un PRD como quality gate antes de pasar a research. Analiza 3 dimensiones: D1 Completitud EAC (Estado Actual Conocido), D2 Claridad EFC + Métricas (Estado Futuro Conocido), D3 Rigor Discovery + Scope. Score ≥7 = PASS. TRIGGERS: analizar calidad PRD, revisar PRD, evaluar PRD, quality gate PRD, "¿está listo el PRD?", "evalúa este PRD", "PRD quality check", "calidad del PRD de..." INTEGRACIÓN: Se ejecuta ANTES de /research (research-from-prd). Score ≥7 = listo para research. ([gemba-app/gemba-product-management-toolkit](https://github.com/gemba-app/gemba-product-management-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/prd-quality-guard) · ★28 · 2 copies)

- **prd-quality-review**: perform a final quality review of a software prd before engineering handoff. use when a prd draft already exists and chatgpt needs to identify ambiguity, contradictions, missing scope decisions, weak acceptance logic, unclear flows, duplicate requirements, and sections that will likely create delivery confusion or rework. suitable as the final review step in a prd workflow. - **prd-quality-rubric**: Critic scoring rubric for drafted specs.
